Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in genomics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 647 and the invariant I(A,B) = 623 mod 4.

Theorem 213.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: For any genomics module M satisfying the spectral property hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 303. The projective resolution has length at most 411 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.
